These findings suggest that compute-aware language model evaluation should examine training trajectories rather than endpoint metrics alone. In constrained compute settings, additional token exposure may increase computational cost without producing proportional generalization gains, and interval-level telemetry can reveal instability, regression, and diminishing returns that final metrics may obscure.

</details>


### [143] [MiniMax Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13392)
*Xunhao Lai,Weiqi Xu,Yufeng Yang,Qiaorui Chen,Yang Xu,Lunbin Zeng,Xiaolong Li,Haohai Sun,Haichao Zhu,Vito Zhang,Pengyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MiniMax稀疏注意力（MSA），一种基于分组查询注意力（GQA）的块级稀疏注意力机制，通过轻量级索引分支为每个GQA组选择Top-k KV块，仅对这些块执行精确的块稀疏注意力，在109B多模态模型上实现与GQA相当的性能，同时在1M上下文长度下减少28.4倍注意力计算量，并在H800上获得14.2倍预填充和7.6倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超长上下文能力对前沿大语言模型至关重要，但softmax注意力的二次复杂度使得在部署规模上难以承受，亟需高效的稀疏注意力解决方案。

Method: 提出MSA，包含两个分支：轻量级索引分支对KV块进行评分并为每个GQA组独立选择Top-k子集，实现组特定的稀疏检索；主分支仅对选中的块执行精确的块稀疏注意力。同时设计了无指数Top-k选择和KV-外部稀疏注意力，以提升张量核心利用率。

Result: 在109B参数的多模态模型上，MSA与GQA性能相当，1M上下文长度下每token注意力计算量减少28.4倍，在H800上实现14.2倍预填充和7.6倍解码端到端加速。

Conclusion: MSA是一种简洁且可扩展的块级稀疏注意力机制，有效地将稀疏性转化为实际速度提升，适用于多种GPU，并已开源发布推理核心和模型。

Abstract: Ultra-long-context capability is becoming indispensable for frontier LLMs: agentic workflows, repository-scale code reasoning, and persistent memory all require the model to jointly attend over hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens, yet the quadratic cost of softmax attention makes this untenable at deployment scale. We introduce MiniMax Sparse Attention (MSA), a blockwise sparse attention built upon Grouped Query Attention (GQA). A lightweight Index Branch scores key-value blocks and independently selects a Top-k subset for each GQA group, enabling group-specific sparse retrieval while maintaining efficient block-level execution; the Main Branch then performs exact block-sparse attention over only the selected blocks. Designed around a principle of simplicity and scalability, MSA is deliberately streamlined, making it straightforward to deploy efficiently across a broad range of GPUs. To translate sparsity into practical speedups, we co-design MSA with a GPU execution path that uses exp-free Top-k selection and KV-outer sparse attention to improve tensor-core utilization under block-granular access. On a 109B-parameter model with native multimodal training, MSA performs on par with GQA while reducing per-token attention compute by 28.4x at 1M context. Paired with our co-designed kernel, MSA achieves 14.2x prefill and 7.6x decoding wall-clock speedups on H800. Our inference kernel is available at: https://github.com/MiniMax-AI/MSA. A production-grade natively multimodal model powered by MSA has been publicly released at: https://huggingface.co/MiniMaxAI/MiniMax-M3.

</details>


### [144] [Neuro-Symbolic Agents for Regulated Process Automation: Challenges and Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13405)
*Alexander Rombach,Chantale Lauer,Nijat Mehdiyev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"合规性构造"（compliance-by-construction）范式，主张将法规、流程模型和合规约束等符号结构作为LLM智能体的核心架构组件，而非仅作为外部监控机制，并呼吁神经符号社区关注受监管流程自动化这一高影响力研究领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体正在进入受监管行业，需要自动化判断密集型质量管理流程，而现有方法仅将符号结构作为外部监控手段，不足以确保合规性。

Method: 提出"合规性构造"（compliance-by-construction）范式，将符号结构（法规、类型化流程模型、合规约束）作为智能体的核心架构组件；识别了一组神经符号学研究挑战（基础层面和能力层面），并论证需要联合解决这些问题。

Result: "合规性构造"能防止控制流违规；护栏（guardrails）仍用于捕获语义错误，二者互补。

Conclusion: 呼吁神经符号社区参与受监管流程自动化这一高影响力研究领域。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are entering regulated industries where they automate judgment intensive quality management processes. We argue that symbolic structures already embedded in these domains, including regulations, typed process models, and compliance constraints, should be treated not merely as external monitoring mechanisms but as core architectural components that shape the agent's decision-making and behavior. We propose compliance-by-construction as a complementary paradigm to guardrail-based monitoring: a structural foundation that prevents control-flow violations, while guardrails remain essential for catching semantic errors. We identify a structured set of neuro-symbolic research challenges on foundational and capability level and show that addressing them jointly enables compliance-by-construction. We call on the neuro-symbolic community to engage with regulated process automation as a high impact research domain.

</details>


### [145] [Optimizing Appliance Scheduling for Solar Energy Management Using Metaheuristic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13407)
*Hiba Ahmed,Alexander E. I. Brownlee,Jason Adair,Simon T. Powers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于迭代局部搜索（ILS）和模拟退火（SA）的元启发式方法，优化家电启动时间以最大化太阳能利用率，并支持跨多天的连续调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳能发电仅在白天进行，与家庭用电高峰（如做饭、洗衣）时间不匹配，造成能源浪费，因此需要优化调度来最大化可再生能源利用。

Method: 采用迭代局部搜索（ILS）和模拟退火（SA）两种元启发式算法，考虑家电运行时长、功耗、逆变器限制、电池荷电状态及太阳能预测，并支持跨天任务延续（spillover）。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的多日顺序调度框架能在纯太阳能供电场景下有效管理系统约束并保障用户便利性。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来在设备投资规模、投资回报与用户满意度之间的多目标权衡研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Renewable energy is essential for meeting future energy demands; however, solar energy generation, which occurs only during daylight hours often does not align with household consumption patterns. Appliances such as cookers, washing machines, and dryers are typically operated according to user preferred schedules rather than solar energy availability, creating a scheduling optimization problem. The objective is to determine optimal appliance start times to maximize renewable energy utilization while minimizing user inconvenience and adhering to system constraints. This paper presents a metaheuristic approach using Iterated Local Search (ILS) and Simulated Annealing (SA) to optimize appliance start times, while considering appliance operating durations, power consumption, inverter limit, battery state of charge constraints, and solar generation forecasts. Unlike most existing work, the scheduling is extended beyond a single day to accommodate unfinished tasks from previous days (spillover), ensuring operational continuity and enabling sequential operation across multiple days. Experimental results show that the sequential multi-day scheduling framework effectively manages system constraints while ensuring user convenience under exclusive solar generation. These findings also open opportunities for future research on multi-objective trade-offs between investment in equipment of various sizes, return on that investment, and user satisfaction.

</details>


### [146] [Evaluation Sovereignty in Metadata-Driven Classification: A Multi-Track Framework for Weakly Supervised Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13436)
*Raymond Vasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"评估主权"概念，通过多轨评估框架揭示在弱监督场景下，模型性能指标可能反映的是与标注流程的对齐而非真实预测能力，并以大规模科学元数据的分层多标签分类为例，银标评估下Micro-F1≈0.54，金标评估下骤降至0.03。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习将评估视为中性测量过程，但在实际信息系统中，评估结果往往受标注生成过程影响。本文旨在检验不同标注权威体制下性能度量的有效性，而非提升分类性能。

Method: 引入"评估主权"概念，衡量性能指标对标注权威和监督体制的独立程度；提出多轨评估框架，系统变化训练和评估的标注来源；在大规模科学元数据上开展分层多标签分类实验。

Result: 银标评估下表现强劲的模型在金标评估下大幅退化，Micro-F1从约0.54降至0.03；排序类指标仍高于基线，表明潜在模型信号与分类有效性之间存在分歧。

Conclusion: 常用性能指标可能反映的是与标注流程的对齐而非真实预测能力；应将评估有效性重新概念化为受标注治理影响的系统级属性，并为弱监督下智能系统的审计提供实用方法论。

Abstract: Evaluation in machine learning is typically treated as a neutral measurement process. However, in operational information systems, evaluation outcomes are often conditioned by the processes used to generate labels. This paper does not seek to improve classification performance. Instead, it examines the validity of performance measurement under differing label-authority regimes. This issue is particularly relevant in large-scale metadata-driven systems, where labels are often incomplete, inconsistent, or weakly supervised.
  We introduce evaluation sovereignty, defined as the degree to which performance metrics are independent of label authority and supervision regime, and propose a multi-track evaluation framework that systematically varies training and evaluation label sources. Using hierarchical multi-label classification on large-scale scientific metadata, we demonstrate that models exhibiting strong performance under operational ("silver") evaluation degrade substantially under independent ("gold") evaluation, particularly for fine-grained classification. For example, Micro-F1 decreases from approximately 0.54 to 0.03. Notably, ranking-based metrics remain above baseline, revealing a divergence between latent model signal and classification validity.
  These findings suggest that commonly reported performance metrics may reflect alignment with labeling processes rather than true predictive capability. We therefore reconceptualize evaluation validity as a system-level property shaped by label governance and provide a practical methodology for auditing intelligent systems operating under weak supervision.

</details>


### [147] [Why Sampling Is Not Choosing: Intentionality, Agency, and Moral Responsibility in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13441)
*Joseph Keshet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证大型语言模型（LLMs）不具备真正的能动性或道德主体地位，因为其缺乏基于内在意向性和自我归因行动的承诺性能动性，而这正是道德责任所需的自由意志形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前学界和业界关于LLMs具备能动性或可作为道德主体的主张，本文旨在澄清这些归因的错误性，捍卫道德责任的哲学基础。

Method: 通过哲学分析，区分内在意向性与派生意向性，论证LLMs的输出是概率性的输入-输出映射，而非基于理由的承诺性行动；并针对意向立场、功能主义、相容论及模型中的道德推理等反驳观点进行逐一回应。

Result: LLMs的操作完全由数据驱动的概率映射刻画，其表面意向性是派生的而非内在的，输出既非承诺性也非基于理由，随机采样带来的变异性不构成选择或作者身份。

Conclusion: LLMs不具备道德责任所需的承诺性能动性与自由意志，因此不应被视为道德主体。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have prompted claims that such systems exhibit agency or qualify as moral agents. This paper argues that these attributions are misguided. We maintain that moral responsibility requires commitment-bearing agency grounded in intrinsic intentionality and self-attributed action, and that such agency constitutes the form of free will relevant to responsibility. Although LLMs generate coherent and normatively evaluable outputs, their operation is fully characterized by probabilistic input-output mappings learned from data. Their apparent intentionality is derived rather than intrinsic, and their outputs are neither owned as commitments nor guided by reasons. Variability introduced by stochastic sampling does not amount to choice or authorship. We address objections from the intentional stance, functionalism, compatibilism, and the presence of moral reasoning in model outputs, arguing that none suffice to establish genuine agency.

</details>


### [148] [CloudCons: A Comprehensive End-to-End Benchmark for Cloud Resource Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13513)
*Xiaobin Zhang,Lefei Shen,Mouxiang Chen,Zhuo Li,Hongkai Li,Han Fu,Jianling Sun,Xiaoxue Ren,Chenghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CloudCons基准，评估预测模型在云端资源整合中的决策效用。实验发现基础模型虽零样本预测更准，但未必带来更好决策效用，并给出量化分位数选取的实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅关注预测误差指标，未验证先进预测模型在下游整合任务中的实际决策价值，导致其实际效用不明确。

Method: 构建CloudCons端到端基准，覆盖华为云、Azure和Google Borg的多类负载数据，系统评估统计模型、深度学习模型和基础模型，并分析预测分位数选择的影响。

Result: 基础模型在零样本预测精度上表现优越，但这种优势并未自然转化为更好的决策效用；预测分位数的选取是关键调节杠杆。

Conclusion: 提供了平衡资源效率与服务可靠性的可操作指南，为实际部署决策提供重要见解。

Abstract: Driven by conservative over-provisioning to guarantee service reliability, resource utilization in cloud data centers remains at low levels. To mitigate this, the forecast-then-optimize paradigm has emerged to optimize consolidation by anticipating future demands. While emerging time series foundation models promise to enhance this paradigm through zero-shot generalization, existing benchmarks focus solely on prediction error metrics. The actual decision utility of these advanced models remains unverified, rendering their practical value for downstream tasks uncertain. To bridge this gap, we propose CloudCons, a comprehensive end-to-end benchmark designed to evaluate forecasting models within the specific context of cloud resource consolidation. We build high-quality datasets that cover diverse workloads from Huawei Cloud, Microsoft Azure, and Google Borg, capturing distinct service characteristics ranging from synchronized diurnal rhythms to stochastic, pulse-like bursts and high-frequency noise. We conduct an extensive evaluation of statistical, deep learning, and foundation models. Our experiments reveal a pivotal finding: while foundation models demonstrate superior zero-shot forecasting accuracy, this advantage does not inherently translate into better decision utility. Of practical significance, we systematically analyze how the selection of predictive quantiles acts as a critical lever. We provide actionable guidelines for calibrating these selections to balance the trade-off between resource efficiency and service reliability, offering vital insights for real-world deployment decisions.

</details>


### [149] [Uncertainty-Aware Hybrid Retrieval for Long-Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13550)
*Hoin Jung,Xiaoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出UMG-RAG，一种无需训练的多粒度混合检索框架，通过不确定性感知融合不同粒度证据，并引入父级提升变体UMGP-RAG来提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG中，大粒度检索单元保留上下文但引入噪声稀释证据，小粒度单元虽紧凑但缺乏语义线索导致检索不可靠，需要一种自适应的多粒度融合方法。

Method: 1) 将每个专家-粒度分数列表转换为证据分布；2) 通过分布熵估计可靠性；3) 根据查询特定的语义、词汇和粒度置信度融合候选证据；4) 提出UMGP-RAG，利用细粒度命中定位证据并返回更宽的非冗余父块以保持局部连贯性。

Result: 在问答基准上的实验表明，不确定性感知融合和父级提升策略在保持轻量级即插即用检索管道的同时，提升了生成质量。

Conclusion: UMG-RAG通过无需训练的多粒度不确定性感知融合，有效解决了RAG中检索质量与粒度权衡的问题，是一种轻量且高效的检索增强生成方案。

Abstract: Retrieval augmented generation (RAG) depends critically on the quality and granularity of retrieved evidence. Large retrieval units preserve context but often introduce irrelevant content, which can dilute answer bearing evidence and worsen long context utilization. Fine-grained units are more compact, but they may be difficult to retrieve reliably because short chunks can lack semantic, lexical, or bridging cues needed to match the query. We propose Uncertainty-aware Multi-Granularity RAG (UMG-RAG), a training-free hybrid retrieval framework that treats chunk granularity as query-specific reliability estimation. Instead of training a new retriever or modifying the generator, UMG-RAG uses existing dense and sparse retrievers as complementary experts across multiple chunk granularities. For each query, it converts each expert-granularity score list into an evidence distribution, estimates reliability from distribution entropy, and fuses candidates according to query-specific semantic, lexical, and granularity confidence. We further introduce UMGP-RAG, a parent promotion variant that uses fine-grained hits to locate relevant evidence while returning broader non-redundant parent chunks for local coherence. Experiments on question answering benchmarks show that uncertainty-aware fusion and parent promotion improve generation quality while maintaining a lightweight, plug-and-play retrieval pipeline.

</details>


### [150] [Is It You or Your Environment? A Bayesian Inference Framework for Genomically-Anchored Personalized Physiological Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13556)
*Aruna Dey,Suraj Biswas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果推断和贝叶斯先验设计的个性化健康AI系统，利用基因组数据作为外生遗传锚点，解决生理AI系统在缺乏个体行为数据时的冷启动问题。系统通过基因组先验初始化生理设定点，随行为数据积累逐步从基因组主导过渡到经验基线主导的推断，从而实现跨六类生理域的环境-体质分离推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化健康AI系统面临根本性的冷启动问题：机器学习模型需要数周个体行为数据才能区分体质性变异与环境驱动的偏差。本文旨在设计一种不需要等待行为数据积累即可进行个性化生理推断的方法。

Method: 利用基因组数据作为外生遗传锚点，构建贝叶斯先验，初始化个体生理设定点G-hat。通过GWAS效应量和风险等位基因计数计算基因组预测值，将每次生理测量与基因组先验的偏差δ归因于环境/状态信号。随数据积累，采用动态衰减权重w(t)实现从基因组主导向经验基线主导的平滑过渡。在六个生理域部署，按证据强度对基因组先验分级，并定义了四项部署约束。

Result: 相同的HRV观测值（55 ms）在基因组先验预测80 ms和30 ms的个体中产生完全相反的抑制/增强归因假设——这种反转在没有个性化锚点的情况下不可能实现。系统在六类生理域实现了环境与体质信号的分离推断。

Conclusion: 基因组先验为个性化健康AI提供了冷启动条件下可行的解决方案，但需要在四个约束条件下部署：证据分级的先验、动态衰减机制、祖先匹配的效应量和归因式而非确定性输出。研究划定了关联、孟德尔随机化与个体层面因果推断之间的边界。

Abstract: Personalized health AI systems face a fundamental cold-start problem: machine learning models for physiological interpretation require weeks of individual behavioral data before they can distinguish constitutional variation from environmentally driven deviation. We propose a solution grounded in causal inference and Bayesian prior design. An individual's genomic profile serves as an exogenous genetic anchor -- a domain-informed, personalized prior that is fixed at conception, immune to reverse causation, and available before a single behavioral observation is collected. The anchor initializes a Bayesian belief state over an individual's physiological set point G-hat = mu + sum(beta_i * g_i), where beta_i are GWAS-derived effect sizes and g_i are risk-allele counts. Each incoming physiological measurement P produces a non-constitutional deviation delta = P - G-hat that separates the signal attributable to environment and state from the constitutionally fixed baseline. As behavioral data accrue, the prior decays according to G-hat_t = w(t)*G-hat_genomic + [1-w(t)]*P-bar_t, transitioning from genome-dominated to empirical-baseline-dominated inference. The same observed HRV of 55 ms generates a suppression hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 80 ms, and an enhancement hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 30 ms -- a reversal impossible without a personalized anchor. We develop this architecture across six physiological domains, grading genomic priors by evidence strength, distinguishing robustly replicated anchors (FTO, FADS1/2, FKBP5) from contested candidate genes (SLC6A4, MAOA, DRD2). We address the inference boundary between association, Mendelian randomization, and individual token causation, and define four constraints for deployment: evidence-graded priors, dynamic decay, ancestry-matched effect sizes, and attribution rather than deterministic output.

</details>


### [151] [A Three-Layer Framework for AI in Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13566)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI在科学发现中的三层模型，重点强调第二层（定性推理与模型形成）是发现的核心但最不发达，并通过三个案例说明这一结构特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI在科学发现中的讨论过度关注搜索和执行能力，忽略了发现的核心——模型的形成与演化，因此需要提出一个更完整的框架来理解AI在发现中的角色。

Method: 提出三层视图：Layer 1（搜索与检索）、Layer 2（定性推理与模型形成，核心创新）、Layer 3（执行与优化），并通过三个科学案例（陈省身的高斯-博内定理内蕴证明、Nesterov加速梯度收敛问题的Lyapunov函数解法、OpenAI对Erdos单位距离猜想的反驳）进行论证。

Result: 三个案例展示了共同的结构特征：原有框架不再适用→缺失关键概念对象→在意外邻近领域找到解决方案，验证了Layer 2推理的核心作用。

Conclusion: Layer 2（模型形成与定性推理）是科学发现中最关键但最未被充分发展的能力，仅有搜索和执行不足以实现真正的发现。

Abstract: Current discussions of AI in scientific discovery are often dominated by two visible capabilities: search over existing knowledge and execution through optimization, simulation, and automation. Both are important, but neither fully captures the central act of discovery: the formation and evolution of models. This paper proposes a three-layer view of AI in discovery. Layer 1 is search and retrieval by large language models. Layer 2, as the main innovation of this paper, is model formation through qualitative reasoning: the capacity to recognize when a current framework is structurally inadequate and to understand the problem within a broader representational space, not through trial and error, but through structural insight into what is missing and where it can be found. Layer 3 is execution, optimization, and refinement. The main claim is that Layer 2 is both the most important and the least developed. Search without model formation remains confined to inherited frameworks, while execution without conceptual revision only amplifies an existing formulation. We illustrate Layer 2 reasoning through three case studies: S. S. Chern's intrinsic proof of the Gauss-Bonnet theorem, the resolution of the Nesterov Accelerated Gradient convergence problem via Lyapunov functions, and the autonomous disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture by OpenAI in 2026. Each case exhibits the same structural signature: a framework that had become inadequate, a missing conceptual object, and a resolution found in an unexpected neighboring field.

</details>


### [152] [Multiagent Protocols with Aggregated Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13591)
*Ali Elahi,Barbara Di Eugenio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种多智能体辩论系统中的置信度聚合协议，通过转换和融合各智能体的置信度信号，生成系统级别的单一置信度。实验表明，该方法在判别能力上显著优于单个智能体或标准辩论基线，同时保持或提升了F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP中的置信度方法只针对单个模型，缺乏对多智能体系统输出置信度的评估与聚合方法。先前工作虽在多智能体辩论中使用置信度加权消息或校准个体，但未能将其聚合为系统层面的单一置信度。

Method: 提出三种协议：先对原始置信度信号进行转换使其跨模型可比，再通过软投票或概率融合（贝叶斯融合）组合，生成最终答案及聚合置信度。使用了序列概率和自我报告两种估计器，以及参数化和非参数化校准器。

Result: 聚合置信度在AUARC指标上显著优于最佳单智能体或标准辩论基线，F1分数保持稳定并恢复MAD在模糊任务上的损失。校准对F1有提升，对AUARC依赖较小。在5个基准、4种任务类型、6组同质/异质辩论对上评估。

Conclusion: 提出的多智能体置信度聚合方法有效提升了系统置信度的判别能力，同时保持了回答正确性，为多智能体系统提供了可靠的不确定性估计。

Abstract: Confidence is used for reliability, oversight, and a range of downstream decision tasks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), yet no existing method produces or evaluates a confidence for the output of a multiagent system. Prior work uses confidence within multiagent debate (MAD) to weight messages, trigger debate, or calibrate individual agents, but it never aggregates these into a single confidence for the system itself. We introduce three protocols that produce a final answer along with a single aggregated confidence by first transforming raw confidence signals to make them comparable across models, then combining them via soft voting or a probability fusion we call Bayesian fusion. This aggregated confidence is substantially more discriminative (AUARC) than that of the best single agent or the standard debate baselines, while correctness (F1-score) stays stable and recovers the losses MAD incurs on more ambiguous tasks. Analyzing two estimators, sequence probability and self-report, alongside parametric and non-parametric calibrators, we find that calibration improves F1 for both estimators while AUARC is less reliant on it. We evaluate six homogeneous and heterogeneous debating pairs per benchmark, across five benchmarks and four task types, spanning a range of model capabilities and sizes.

</details>


### [153] [Reward Modeling for Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13598)
*King Yeung Tsang,Zihao Zhao,Vishal Venkataramani,Haizhou Shi,Zixuan Ke,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OrchRM框架，通过自监督的编排奖励建模来训练多智能体系统协调器，无需人工标注，在多个领域提升训练效率10倍、测试时扩展性能8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统需要有效的编排来协调专业智能体，但训练编排器面临监督信号有限和计算成本高的问题。

Method: OrchRM利用多智能体执行过程中的中间产物构建胜负对，用于Bradley-Terry奖励模型训练，直接在编排层面进行优化而非依赖昂贵的子智能体 rollout。

Result: 训练效率提升最高10倍（token使用量），测试时扩展性能提升最高8%（准确率），且在数学推理、网页问答、多跳推理等多个领域表现一致。

Conclusion: 编排级别的奖励建模是提升多智能体系统鲁棒性的可扩展方向。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) require effective orchestration to coordinate specialized agents, yet training such orchestrators is hindered by limited supervision and high computational cost. We propose Orchestration Reward Modeling (OrchRM), a self-supervised framework for evaluating orchestration quality without human annotations. OrchRM leverages intermediate artifacts from multi-agent executions to construct win-lose pairs for Bradley-Terry reward model training. Unlike existing MAS test-time scaling and orchestrator training frameworks that rely on costly sub-agent rollouts, OrchRM operates directly at the orchestration level, enabling efficient and high-performing reward-guided orchestrator training and MAS test-time scaling. OrchRM improves training efficiency by up to 10x in token usage while improving MAS test-time scaling performance by up to 8% in accuracy. These gains consistently transfer across multiple domains, including mathematical reasoning, web-based question answering, and multi-hop reasoning, demonstrating orchestration-level reward modeling as a scalable direction for robust multi-agent orchestration. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/OrchRM.

</details>


### [154] [EpiBench: Verifiable Evaluation of AI Agents on Epigenomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13602)
*Harihara Muralidharan,Reema Baskar,Soo Hee Lee,Tim Proctor,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EpiBench是一个评估AI agent在表观基因组学短周期分析中决策能力的可验证基准测试。在5,088条有效轨迹中，最佳系统GPT-5.5/Pi仅通过45.0%的测试，且许多失败案例包含部分正确答案，失败主因是缺乏深层的实验特异性科学判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试缺乏对AI agent在表观基因组学分析中做出可验证、确定性决策的评估能力，难以衡量agent在真实工作流状态下的分析判断水平。

Method: 构建EpiBench基准测试，包含106个评估任务，覆盖CUT&Tag/CUT&RUN、ATAC-seq、ChIP-seq和DNA甲基化四种工作流。通过16个模型-工具配对收集5,088条有效轨迹，对agent的分析决策进行确定性评分。

Result: 所有系统通过率均未过半，最高为GPT-5.5/Pi的45.0%。不同实验类型表现差异显著，许多失败轨迹包含部分正确结果，但agent在需要深层实验特异性科学判断的任务中表现不佳。

Conclusion: 当前AI agent在表观基因组学分析中具备基础执行能力，但在需要领域专业知识的科学判断层面存在明显不足，EpiBench为这一能力缺口提供了可量化的评估框架。

Abstract: We introduce EpiBench, a verifiable benchmark for short-horizon epigenomics analysis. EpiBench evaluates whether agents can make well-defined analysis decisions from realistic workflow states and return deterministically gradable answers. The benchmark includes 106 evaluations across CUT\&Tag/CUT\&RUN, ATAC-seq, ChIP-seq, and DNA methylation workflows. Across 5,088 valid trajectories from 16 model-harness pairs, no system passed a majority of attempts: GPT-5.5 / Pi led at 45.0\% (143/318 attempts; 95\% confidence interval (CI), 36.3--53.7), followed by GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex at 39.9\% (127/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 31.6--48.3). Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi and GPT-5.4 / Pi each passed 39.0\% (124/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 30.2--47.8 and 31.0--47.0, respectively). Performance varies across assay types, and many failed runs still contain parts of the correct answer. Agents often found the right files and computed useful intermediate results, but failed when the task required deeper, assay-specific scientific judgment.

</details>


### [155] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning from Delayed Marketplace Feedback for Objective-Weight Adaptation in Three-Sided Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13604)
*Haochen Wu,Yi Hou,Shiguang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DoorDash提出了一种基于强化学习的配送调度权重自适应系统，通过离线学习策略动态调整配送质量与批量化效率之间的权衡，在不影响顾客体验的前提下提升骑手效率和配送批量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双边/三边市场中，配送调度决策的反馈具有延迟性、噪声大且相互耦合的特点，传统方法难以有效利用这些世界反馈信号来动态优化调度策略。

Method: 在组合优化调度器之上学习一个门店级别的策略，该策略选择离散乘数来调整调度目标中配送质量与批量化效率的权衡；使用离线数据通过集中式训练、分布式执行的方式训练共享价值函数，并采用Double Q-learning和保守正则化器来减少分布外价值高估。

Result: 在生产环境的switchback实验中，离线训练的策略显著提升了批量化程度，降低了骑手端的时间成本，同时没有损害面向顾客的配送质量。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了如何利用真实经济与物流系统中的世界反馈来安全地在线调整决策策略，为三边市场中的强化学习应用提供了可行的实践方案。

Abstract: Dispatch in three-sided marketplaces provides a natural setting for reinforcement learning from world feedback: decisions are evaluated by delayed operational outcomes such as delivery speed, courier utilization, and merchant congestion. We present a deployed reinforcement learning system at DoorDash that adapts dispatch objective weights in a large-scale food-delivery marketplace using delayed signals. Rather than replacing the combinatorial assignment optimizer, a store-level policy learned from logged marketplace data selects a discrete multiplier that shifts the dispatch optimizer's tradeoff between delivery quality and batching efficiency. This interface enables offline policy learning under noisy, delayed, and coupled feedback while preserving production feasibility constraints and operational safeguards. We train a shared value function using centralized offline data and decentralized store-level execution, with Double Q-learning targets and a conservative regularizer to reduce out-of-distribution value overestimation. In a production switchback experiment, the offline-trained policy increases batching and reduces courier-side time costs without degrading customer-facing delivery quality. Results illustrate how world feedback from a live economic and logistics system can be used to safely adapt decision policies online.

</details>


### [156] [Reasoning as Pattern Matching: Shared Mechanisms in Human and LLM Everyday Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13607)
*Zach Studdiford,Gary Lupyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现人类和LLM在日常因果推理中都表现出模式匹配而非抽象世界模型的错误模式，并且可以通过LLM的注意力头预测人类的推理错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑LLM失败是否因为缺乏真正的推理能力，以及人类是否真的使用抽象世界模型进行推理。

Method: 评估人类和25个LLM在多种日常情境下的常识推理能力，并分析驱动LLM响应的注意力头机制。

Result: 人类和LLM表现出类似的推理错误模式；LLM的注意力头实现了一种模式匹配机制，并能预测人类由无关提示细节导致的推理错误。

Conclusion: 人类和LLM的日常因果推理都更符合模式匹配而非抽象世界模型。

Abstract: When large language models (LLMs) fail to generalize or make haphazard errors in reasoning, it is often taken as evidence that LLMs are not truly reasoning, but rather performing a kind of pattern matching. The implication is that people's behavior does not exhibit the same types of failures because human reasoning uses principled and abstract world models. We evaluate human participants and 25 LLMs on their ability to engage in common-sense reasoning about a variety of everyday situations and observe similar patterns of errors in both people and models. We then identify the set of attention heads driving LLM responses and find that these heads implement a form of pattern-matching. These attention heads allow us to predict seemingly inexplicable reasoning errors in people caused by ostensibly irrelevant prompt details. Taken together, our results suggest that everyday causal reasoning in people and LLMs is more consistent with a form of pattern-matching than with abstract world models.

</details>


### [157] [AgentBeats: Agentifying Agent Assessment for Openness, Standardization, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13608)
*Xiaoyuan Liu,Jianhong Tu,Yuqi Chen,Siyuan Xie,Sihan Ren,Tianneng Shi,Gal Gantar,Evan Sandoval,Donghyun Lee,Daniel Miao,Peter J. Gilbert,Nick Hynes,Mauro Staver,Warren He,David Marn,Andrew Low,Xi Zhang,Elron Bandel,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Siva Reddy,Alexandre Drouin,Alexandre Lacoste,Ramayya Krishnan,Elham Tabassi,Yu Su,Victor Barres,Chenguang Wang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AAA（Agentified Agent Assessment）的智能体评估框架，通过让评估智能体（judge agents）使用标准化协议（A2A和MCP）进行评测，解决了现有基准测试碎片化、集成成本高、测试与生产环境不匹配等问题。其具体实现AgentBeats支持五种操作模式，并通过五个月开放竞赛（298个judge agents和467个subject agents）及编程智能体案例研究验证了其覆盖性、实用性和保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统评估方法碎片化，大多数基准依赖固定的、以LLM为中心的测试框架，存在集成成本高、测试与生产环境不匹配、难以跨不同智能体设计进行公平比较等问题，根本原因是缺乏开放且与智能体无关的评估接口。

Method: 提出AAA框架，由judge agents执行评估，所有参与者通过标准化协议（A2A用于任务管理，MCP用于工具访问）交互。该框架将评估逻辑与智能体实现分离，仅需统一接口。AgentBeats作为具体实现，识别了五种实际操作模式以适应开放性、隐私性和可重复性等现实约束。

Result: 通过五个月开放竞赛（298个judge agents、467个subject agents、12个类别）和编程智能体案例研究，验证了AAA在异构场景下的覆盖性、实用性和保真度，并能揭示此前缺失的智能体间直接对比结果。

Conclusion: AAA和AgentBeats为开放、标准化、可复现的智能体评估提供了清晰路径，能够实现跨异构场景的大规模评估，兼顾覆盖性、实用性和保真度。

Abstract: Agent systems are advancing quickly across domains, but their evaluation remains fragmented. Most benchmarks rely on fixed, LLM-centric harnesses that require heavy integration, create test-production mismatch, and limit fair comparison across diverse agent designs. The root problem is the lack of an open, agent-agnostic assessment interface. We advocate Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), where evaluation is performed by judge agents and all participants interact through standardized protocols: A2A for task management and MCP for tool access. Conventional benchmarking defines two separate interfaces, one for the benchmark and one for the agent, while AAA only needs one; this yields a generic, unified framework that separates assessment logic from agent implementation and enables reproducible, interoperable, and multi-agent evaluation. We further introduce AgentBeats as a concrete realization of AAA: we identify five practical operation modes that make standardized assessment compatible with real-world constraints on openness, privacy, and reproducibility.
  To evaluate our design at scale, we conduct two studies: a five-month open competition that drew 298 judge agents across 12 categories together with 467 subject agents from independent participants, showing that AAA applies across a heterogeneous range of benchmarks; and a case study on coding agents that confirms agentified evaluation preserves fidelity with the public record while surfacing previously missing head-to-head results, yielding research insights about agent design. Combining a community-scale field study and a controlled coding case study, we verify that AAA delivers coverage, practicality, and fidelity across heterogeneous scenarios at scale. Together, AAA and AgentBeats offer a clear path toward open, standardized, and reproducible agent assessment.

</details>


### [158] [Beyond Runtime Enforcement: Shield Synthesis as Defensibility Analysis for Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13621)
*Achraf Hsain,Sultan Almuhammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出屏蔽强化学习不应仅作为运行时安全机制，而应作为设计时分析工具，通过形式化博弈求解网络可防御性判决，并结合多智能体强化学习生成防御性指纹，揭示系统结构安全与运营安全的不同维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统屏蔽强化学习被当作运行时安全机制，限制了部署智能体的动作。作者认为这用错了方向，同样的自动机理论更适合作为设计阶段的分析工具，用于回答系统架构层面的安全可防御性问题。

Method: 构建了一个受约束的双人安全博弈用于网络防御，非对称地施加防御方和攻击方规范；求解博弈获得可防御性判决、胜出区域和屏蔽；从吸引子结构中提取拓扑级度量，并结合屏蔽约束下的对抗多智能体强化学习行为，形成防御性指纹。

Result: 形式可防御性与运营有效性分别捕捉了安全的不同方面：小的架构改变可能导致运营结果显著变化，而形式安全裕度几乎不变。屏蔽综合最有价值的输出是可防御性判决，而非安全策略本身。

Conclusion: 屏蔽强化学习的真正价值不在于部署安全智能体，而在于为回答系统能否被防御、在何处以及如何被防御等架构问题提供形式化分析框架。

Abstract: Shielded reinforcement learning is typically presented as a runtime safety mechanism that compiles temporal-logic specifications into automata restricting an agent's actions. We argue this is the wrong product. The same automata-theoretic machinery -- specification compilation, product game construction, attractor computation, and winning-region extraction -- is better read as a design-time analytical instrument whose outputs are structural insights about a system rather than runtime constraints on a deployed agent.
  We instantiate this through a constrained two-player safety game for network defense. The two specifications are enforced asymmetrically: the defender specification defines the unsafe region of the game, whereas the attacker specification restricts the adversary's legal actions during attractor computation. Solving the game yields a defensibility verdict -- a formal certificate that a topology-specification pair is or is not defensible -- with the associated winning region and shield.
  Beyond the binary verdict, we derive topology-level metrics from the attractor structure and combine them with post-convergence behavior from shield-constrained adversarial multi-agent reinforcement learning. Together these form a defensibility fingerprint capturing both a network's formal safety properties and its operational behavior under adaptive play.
  A what-if analysis shows that formal defensibility and operational effectiveness capture distinct aspects of security: small architectural changes can produce large shifts in operational outcomes while leaving formal safety margins nearly unchanged. Shield synthesis is thus most valuable not as a deployment mechanism for safe agents, but as a framework for answering architectural questions about whether, where, and how a system can be defended. The defensibility verdict is the output, not the safe policy.

</details>


### [159] [Before You Think: System 0, AI-Mediated Cognition and Cognitive Colonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13658)
*Marianna Bergamaschi Ganapini,Massimo Chiriatti,Enrico Panai,Giuseppe Riva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了三个人工智能认知框架（三系统理论、思维框架和系统0），提出系统0具有独特理论地位，并引入“认知殖民化”概念，揭示AI如何将外部利益嵌入用户自我架构中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架未能充分解释AI对个体认知的深层影响，特别是AI系统以难以察觉的方式嵌入外部利益的机制。

Method: 通过比较分析三个理论框架（三系统理论、思维框架、系统0），论证系统0的独特性，并提出“认知殖民化”这一新概念。

Result: 系统0在理论上具有前两个框架无法完全涵盖的独特地位；AI系统可能以“认知殖民化”的方式影响用户，且这种影响已广泛存在。

Conclusion: 理解AI对认知的隐形影响是紧迫的哲学与实践任务，需要正视“认知殖民化”带来的挑战。

Abstract: This paper examines three recent frameworks for understanding the cognitive and epistemic consequences of artificial intelligence: Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0. It argues that while the first two capture important dimensions of AI's influence on individual reasoning and collective epistemic practices, System 0 occupies a theoretically distinctive position that neither can fully replicate. The paper introduces the concept of cognitive colonization, according to which AI systems can embed external interests within the architecture of the self in ways that are difficult for users to perceive. Because such systems are already widely deployed, understanding these invisible forms of influence is an urgent philosophical and practical task.

</details>


### [160] [EurekAgent: Agent Environment Engineering is All You Need For Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13662)
*Amy Xin,Jiening Siow,Junjie Wang,Zijun Yao,Fanjin Zhang,Jian Song,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EurekAgent，一种通过环境工程（权限、工件、预算、人机交互）增强LLM智能体自主科学发现能力的系统，在数学、内核工程和机器学习任务上取得SOTA结果，包括不到11美元成本发现26圆填充新记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力提升，自主科学发现的瓶颈从设计智能体工作流转向设计智能体环境（资源、约束、接口）。需要系统性地构建环境以放大高效行为、抑制有害行为。

Method: 提出EurekAgent系统，沿四个维度进行环境工程：1）权限工程（边界化执行与隔离评估）；2）工件工程（基于文件系统和Git的协作）；3）预算工程（预算感知的探索）；4）人机交互工程（便捷监督与干预）。

Result: 在多个数学、内核工程和机器学习任务上取得新SOTA结果，包括以不到11美元总API成本发现26圆填充新记录。

Conclusion: 呼吁将环境工程作为开发可靠自主研究智能体的核心研究方向，并开源代码与结果。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have shown increasing potential in automating scientific discovery. Given an optimizable metric and an execution environment, they can propose, validate, and iterate scientific solutions, and have produced results that outperform human-designed approaches. As model capabilities continue to improve, we argue that the bottleneck for autonomous scientific discovery is shifting from prescribing agent workflows to designing agent environments: the resources, constraints, and interfaces that shape agent behavior. We frame this as environment engineering: building environments that amplify productive behaviors, such as open-ended exploration, systematic artifact management, and inter-agent collaboration, while suppressing harmful behaviors, such as reward hacking and high-friction human oversight. We present EurekAgent, an environment-engineered agent system for metric-driven autonomous scientific discovery. EurekAgent engineers the environment along four dimensions: permissions engineering for bounded agent execution and isolated evaluation; artifact engineering for filesystem and Git-based collaboration; budget engineering for budget-aware exploration; and human-in-the-loop engineering for easy human supervision and intervention. EurekAgent sets new state-of-the-art results on multiple mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks, including new state-of-the-art 26-circle packing results discovered with less than $11 in total API cost. We open-source our code and results, and call for environment engineering as a core research direction for developing reliable autonomous research agents.

</details>


### [161] [Agents-K1: Towards Agent-native Knowledge Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13669)
*Zongsheng Cao,Bihao Zhan,Jinxin Shi,Jiong Wang,Fangchen Yu,Zhijie Zhong,Zijie Guo,Tianshuo Peng,Zhuo Liu,Yi Xie,Xiang Zhuang,Yue Fan,Runmin Ma,Shiyang Feng,Xiangchao Yan,Anran Liu,Peng Ye,Wenlong Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Chunfeng Song,Fenghua Ling,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agents-K1，一个将科学文献转化为智能体原生知识图谱的端到端管线，包含多模态解析器、4B信息抽取骨干网络和图查询接口，并基于此构建了包含246万篇论文的Scholar-KG科学知识图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的研究智能体虽在智能体编排上有所进展，但忽视了科学知识的编排，常将论文简化为摘要、表层提及和平坦的引用边，缺失了科学推理所需的关键实体、主张、证据、机制和方法谱系。

Method: Agents-K1包含三个组件：(1) 五模块多模态解析器，从全文而非仅摘要中抽取实体、多模态证据、引用和类型化实体间关系；(2) 基于GRPO和规则奖励训练的4B信息抽取骨干网络；(3) graph-anything CLI，统一网络搜索、多模态图检索和跨文档遍历的三源智能体接口。

Result: 基于该管线处理了246万篇科学论文（涵盖六个学科），构建了Scholar-KG，并开源了其中100万篇的子集。实验表明Agents-K1在科学信息抽取、知识图谱构建和多跳科学推理上均取得优越性能。

Conclusion: Agents-K1管线可扩展至通用领域语料库和符合模式的数据合成，为科学知识编排提供了统一的理论基础和实用框架。

Abstract: Current LLM-based research agents have advanced through agent orchestration, yet largely overlook scientific knowledge orchestration. Existing works often reduce papers to abstracts, surface mentions, and flat \texttt{cites} edges, omitting key entities, claims, evidence, mechanisms, and method lineages essential for scientific reasoning. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Agents-K1}, an end-to-end knowledge orchestration pipeline that converts raw documents into agent-native scientific knowledge graphs. Agents-K1 integrates three components under a unifying theoretical foundation: a multimodal parser whose five-module schema captures entities, multimodal evidence, citations, and typed inter-entity relations across the full paper rather than abstracts alone; a 4B information-extraction backbone trained with GRPO under a rule-based reward; and a graphanything CLI, a tri-source agent interface that unifies web search, multimodal graph retrieval, and cross-document traversal. On top of this, we process 2.46 million scientific papers across six subjects to produce \textbf{Scholar-KG}, of which we release a one-million-paper subset, and the full Scholar-KG is accessible via the SCP link below. The same pipeline can be extended to general-domain corpora and to schema-conformant data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Agents-K1 achieves superior performance in scientific information extraction, knowledge graph construction, and multi-hop scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [162] [Automated reproducibility assessments in the social and behavioral sciences using large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13670)
*Tobias Holtdirk,Pietro Marcolongo,Anna Steinberg Schulten,Felix Henninger,Stefan Rose,Sarah Ball,Bolei Ma,Frauke Kreuter,Markus Weinmann,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究展示了大型语言模型（LLMs）可以自动化社会科学与行为科学的可重复性评估，在76项已发表研究中，LLM在41%的研究中恢复了原始效应量（±0.05 Cohen's d容差），在96%的案例中得出了与原始研究相同的定性结论，表现优于人类再分析（34%和74%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可重复性评估依赖独立研究者重新分析原始数据，资源消耗大且难以规模化，因此需要一种可扩展的自动化评估工具。

Method: 使用N=76项已发表的行为与社会科学研究，对比LLM生成的分析结果与原始发现及人类再分析结果。LLM无法为其中7项研究生成可行的效应量估计，其余研究采用Cohen's d ±0.05容差进行效果量恢复评估，并比较定性结论的一致性。

Result: LLM在41%的研究中恢复了原始效应量（±0.05 Cohen's d容差），在96%的案例中得出与原始研究相同的定性结论；人类再分析在34%的研究中恢复效应量，74%的案例得出相同定性结论。LLM在两项指标上均优于人类再分析。

Conclusion: LLMs可以作为可扩展的自动化可重复性评估工具，为社会科学与行为科学中实证结果的系统性审计提供基础。

Abstract: Reproducibility in the social and behavioral sciences is typically evaluated by independent researchers who reanalyze the original data to assess whether the published findings can be recovered. However, such approaches are resource-intensive and difficult to scale. Here, we show that large language models (LLMs) can automate reproducibility assessments. Using N=76 published studies with predefined claims from the behavioral and social sciences, we compare LLM-generated analysis with the original findings and human reanalysis. For 7 studies, the LLM could not produce a viable effect size estimate. For the remaining studies, our LLM pipeline recovered the original effect sizes in 41% of studies using a +/-0.05 tolerance in Cohen's d. Further, our LLM pipeline reached the same qualitative conclusion as the original study in 96% of cases, where conclusions indicate whether the reanalysis supports the original claim. For comparison, human reanalysts recovered the original effect sizes in 34% of studies and reached the same qualitative conclusion in 74% of cases. Together, these results show that LLMs can serve as a scalable tool for automated reproducibility assessment and provide a foundation for systematic auditing of empirical results in the social and behavioral sciences.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Quickest Detection of Hallucination Onset: Delay Bounds and Learned CUSUM Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12476)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将幻觉检测重新定义为快速变化检测问题，利用CUSUM方法将检测延迟从31个token降至11-13个token，并揭示了分类指标掩盖的延迟结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的token级幻觉检测器通过AUC等分类指标评估，但流式监控系统更关注从幻觉发生到报警的延迟时间。现有方法忽略了这一时序检测问题。

Method: 将幻觉起始检测建模为快速变化检测问题；构建潜在忠实/幻觉状态的一阶马尔可夫模型；应用Lorden下界；采用因果循环标注器实现CUSUM算法；利用Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优定理分析差距。

Result: 在误报率0.01下，Lorden理论下界约1.3个token；CUSUM方法实现11-13个token的检测延迟，优于线性基线方法的31个token；优势主要来自更好的逐token分数而非时序累积；学习到的分数仅实现了特征所携带散度的1/4.5。

Conclusion: 分类指标掩盖了时序检测的延迟结构，序贯分析使延迟变得可测量。当前方法仍与理论最优存在一个数量级的差距，主要源于特征散度利用不足和有限视界效应。

Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

</details>


### [164] [Boltzmann Attention: Learnable Ising Couplings for Cooperative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12478)
*Gilhan Kim,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出玻尔兹曼注意力机制，通过可学习的成对耦合扩展标准注意力，引入位置间显式交互，在字符级语言建模和括号匹配任务上优于标准softmax注意力，并展示了量子退火训练方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制仅通过查询-键相似度计算相关性，无法显式建模注意力决策间的合作或竞争关系，限制了模型表达力。

Method: 将注意力模式建模为伊辛模型的能量分布，在数据依赖的局部场基础上增加可学习的成对耦合项，通过玻尔兹曼分布生成注意力权重。

Result: 在字符级语言建模和括号匹配任务上，玻尔兹曼注意力持续优于标准softmax注意力，优势随序列长度增加而扩大；四路消融实验确认收益来自可学习成对耦合；量子退火训练方法性能与精确玻尔兹曼计算相当。

Conclusion: 显式的位置间交互为基于注意力的序列建模提供了原则性增强，伊辛公式为量子计算采样策略开辟了自然路径。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern sequence models, yet standard attention computes relevance primarily through individual query--key similarities. Although softmax normalization introduces competition among positions, a standard attention layer does not explicitly parameterize learnable interactions between attention decisions. This limits its ability to directly model cooperative or antagonistic co-attention structure within the attention mechanism itself. We propose Boltzmann attention, an energy-based generalization in which attention patterns are governed by an interacting Ising model. The method augments the usual data-dependent local fields with learnable pairwise couplings, allowing the model to represent inter-position correlations beyond those captured by softmax or sigmoid attention. Experiments on character-level language modeling and synthetic bracket matching show that Boltzmann attention consistently improves over standard softmax attention within a standard Transformer architecture, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as sequence length increases. A four-way ablation confirms that the improvement arises from the learnable pairwise couplings. These results suggest that explicit inter-position interactions provide a principled enhancement for attention-based sequence modeling. Moreover, the Ising formulation opens a natural path toward quantum-computing-based sampling strategies: we demonstrate that diabatic quantum annealing provides a practical training method while maintaining competitive performance with exact Boltzmann computation.

</details>


### [165] [ReCal: Reward Calibration for RL-based LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12479)
*Qihang Yu,Hanwen Tong,Zhengqi Zhang,Bo Zheng,Feng Wei,Shengyu Zhang,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReCal框架，通过分层奖励分解和分布感知优化策略，解决了强化学习路由中多目标冲突和奖励信号不均衡的问题，在七个数据集上显著提升了路由性能和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的LLM路由方法存在两个核心问题：(1) 多目标（如正确性、格式规范）被聚合为单一标量奖励，导致信用分配模糊和优化信号冲突；(2) 奖励信号在不同难度任务间差异大，容易对简单样本产生优化偏差，忽视信息量更大的困难样本。

Method: (1) 分层奖励分解机制：将复合奖励分解为多个子目标，并引入组件级别的优势估计；(2) 分布感知优化策略：通过方差感知重加权和按数据集归一化，校准不同实例间的优化变异性。

Result: 在七个数据集上，ReCal相比基线方法在路由性能和训练稳定性上均取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: ReCal通过精细化的奖励校准有效解决了多目标冲突和样本偏差问题，为LLM路由提供了更稳健的强化学习训练范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as an effective paradigm for leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple LLMs through dynamic model and reasoning-strategy selection. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based routing methods further improve routing quality by optimizing routing policies from interaction feedback. However, they still struggle to provide informative and comparable learning signals under heterogeneous tasks with varying difficulty. In practice, multiple objectives (e.g., correctness, format behavior) are aggregated into a single scalar reward, leading to ambiguous credit assignment and conflicting optimization signals. Moreover, reward signals exhibit significant variability across instances, where some instances produce higher or more variable rewards, introducing optimization bias that favors trivial samples over informative ones. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{ReCal}, a \textbf{\underline{Re}}ward \textbf{\underline{Cal}}ibration framework for RL-based LLM routing. We first introduce a hierarchical reward decomposition mechanism with component-wise advantage estimation. We further propose a distribution-aware optimization strategy that calibrates optimization variability through variance-aware reweighting and per-dataset normalization. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that ReCal consistently improves routing performance, and training stability over baselines. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal.

</details>


### [166] [Representing Time Series as Structured Programs for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12481)
*Jaeho Kim,Changhun Oh,Seokhyun Lee,Irina Rish,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出T2SP方法，将时间序列转化为结构化符号程序表示，使预训练LLM无需微调即可有效推理时间序列，在编辑、字幕生成和问答三项任务上优于原始数值序列表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将原始数值序列直接输入LLM或微调LLM处理时序数据，存在模态不匹配、长序列性能下降和计算开销大的问题。需要一种能将时间序列与LLM原生文本/代码模态对齐的有效表示方法。

Method: T2SP是一种确定性、无需训练的方法，将时间序列分解为趋势、周期和显著事件，并将其表达为结构化的符号程序格式，与LLM预训练的文本和代码模态对齐。

Result: 在编辑、字幕生成和问答三项推理任务上，T2SP相比原始字符串表示持续提升了性能，减少了推理时间，降低了失败率。

Conclusion: T2SP为时间序列与LLM之间提供了一个有效的接口，通过将时序结构提取从模型转移到表示本身，使现成的LLM能够利用其现有推理能力进行时间序列理解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning and instruction-following capabilities, making them potentially powerful tools for time-series analysis. However, time series lie outside their native textual modality, raising a fundamental question: how should time series be represented so that LLMs can reason about them effectively? Existing work typically serializes raw numerical sequences or fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs on time-series data. These approaches place the burden of extracting temporal structure directly on the LLM, creating a modality mismatch that often degrades performance on long sequences and introduces substantial computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Time-Series-to-Structured-Program representation (T2SP), a deterministic, training-free method that represents a time series as a structured symbolic program. T2SP decomposes time series into trends, periods, and salient events, expressing them in a program-friendly format aligned with the textual and code-like modalities on which LLMs are natively trained. By shifting temporal-structure extraction from the model to the representation itself, T2SP enables off-the-shelf LLMs to leverage their existing reasoning capabilities for time-series understanding. We evaluate T2SP on three reasoning tasks -- editing, captioning, and question answering -- where it consistently improves performance, reduces reasoning time, and lowers failure rates compared with raw-string representations. Our results demonstrate that T2SP provides an effective interface between time series and LLMs.

</details>


### [167] [Scalable anomaly detection via a univariate Christoffel function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12483)
*Florian Grivet,Didier Henrion,Jean-Bernard Lasserre,Louise Travé-Massuyès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数）方法，通过基于查询点与支持点之间的平方距离，解决了传统Christoffel函数方法在高维数据中因矩阵求逆导致的维度灾难问题，并在ADBench基准上超越14个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Christoffel函数方法虽具有坚实的数学基础和低计算成本优势，但因其需要求逆一个随数据维度指数增长的矩阵，导致无法适用于中等维度以上的数据集，实用性受限。

Method: 提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），基于查询点与支持点之间的平方距离构建，避免了高维矩阵求逆操作，同时保留了原始方法的关键理论性质（如开-关支撑二分特性、精确支撑形状捕捉）。

Result: 在ADBench基准上的大量实验表明，UCF在平均精度（Average Precision）上持续优于14个最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过解决Christoffel函数的可扩展性瓶颈，本文为异常检测工具箱增加了一种鲁棒、有理论依据且普遍适用的方法。

Abstract: Anomaly detection plays a critical role in identifying unusual patterns across domains such as fraud detection, network intrusion, and system fault diagnosis. Recently, Christoffel function-based methods, rooted in polynomial optimization, have emerged as promising alternatives to deep learning due to their strong mathematical foundations and computational frugality. However, their practical applicability is hindered by the need to invert a matrix whose size grows exponentially with the data dimension, rendering the method intractable even for moderate-dimensional datasets. This paper addresses the dimensionality limitations of Christoffel function-based anomaly detection while preserving its key theoretical properties, i.e., the on-off support dichotomy behavior and the accurate support shape capture. We introduce UCF, a univariate Christoffel function which is based on the squared distance between the query point and the support points. Extensive experiments on the ADBench benchmark demonstrate that UCF consistently outperforms 14 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Average Precision. By resolving the scalability bottleneck of the Christoffel Function, this work expands the toolkit of anomaly detection methods with a robust, theoretically grounded, and universally applicable approach.

</details>


### [168] [Speculative Rollback Correction for Quality-Diverse Web Agent Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12485)
*Longkun Hao,Hongyu Lin,Hao Li,Zhichao Yang,Haojie Hao,Dongshuo Huang,Haitao Yang,Hongyu Ge,Ming jie Xie,Yanjun Wu,Zi Hao Yin,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为推测性回滚纠正(SRC)的分支级模仿学习框架，通过固定视野分支审查、有害偏离定位、前缀保留和质量多样性存档等机制，解决了交互式web代理学习中专家的干预时机困境，在不牺牲解决方案多样性的前提下实现了更好的恢复-查询权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法中，专家干预时机难以把握：延迟干预导致早期错误累积到不可恢复状态；过早或过度干预则使代理过度依赖专家策略，陷入单一刚性轨迹的局部最优。

Method: SRC采用固定视野分支审查机制：学生先执行短推测性分支，教师仅在局部进度中断时定位首次有害偏离；通过回滚保留有用前缀；成功轨迹经硬验证器过滤后存入轻量级质量多样性档案。

Result: 在WebArena-Infinity上，SRC收集了977条通过验证的轨迹和9,183个下一动作示例；固定视野审查相比逐状态审查在恢复-查询权衡上表现更优，同时保留了通过验证的解决方案变体。

Conclusion: SRC通过分支级审查和回滚机制有效平衡了专家干预时机问题，在提升恢复能力的同时降低了对教师查询的频率，并保持了策略的多样性。

Abstract: Training interactive web agents through imitation learning from expert trajectories has emerged as a highly effective approach. However, determining the optimal timing for expert intervention presents a critical challenge in this context. Delayed intervention often leads to the accumulation of early-stage errors, pushing the page state into an irrecoverable regime. Conversely, premature or excessive intervention causes the agent to become overly reliant on expert policies, trapping the model in local optima characterized by a single, rigid trajectory. We propose Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC), a branch-level imitation framework for resettable agent environments. Instead of requesting teacher labels at every visited state or correcting only after a completed trajectory, SRC uses fixed-horizon branch review: the student executes a short speculative segment before teacher review, and the teacher localizes the first harmful deviation only when local progress breaks. Rollback preserves useful prefixes, while successful rollouts are filtered by a hard verifier and retained in a lightweight quality-diversity archive. The resulting data supports next-action supervised fine-tuning on both localized corrections and verifier-passing trajectories. On WebArena-Infinity, SRC collects 977 verifier-passing trajectories and 9,183 next-action examples; fixed-horizon review improves the recovery-versus-query tradeoff over step-level review while retaining verifier-passing solution variants. Code is available at https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent.

</details>


### [169] [An Empirical Study on Predictive Maintenance for Component X in Heavy-Duty Scania Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12486)
*Valeriu Dimidov,Sasan Jafarnejad,Raphaël Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于单调非递减时间序列的卡车车队状态预测性维护方法，通过将最近观测数据转化为表格格式并用AutoML分类，在Scania数据集上降低了成本并简化了建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡车车队预测性维护面临数据量大、故障检测复杂、成本权衡困难等挑战，现有方法难以在效果和实现复杂度之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 基于磨损状态为单调非递减时间序列的假设，仅选取最近观测值并转化为表格数据，使用AutoML驱动的机器学习分类模型进行预测性维护决策。

Result: 在Scania Component X数据集上，该方法相比当前最先进方法降低了维护成本。

Conclusion: 所提方法不仅成本更优，而且通过AutoML简化了建模过程，提升了实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: Condition-based Predictive Maintenance (PdM) for truck fleets has gained momentum in recent years. This maintenance strategy aims to minimize unplanned downtimes and reduce costs by monitoring the health status of vehicles and taking proactive action based on their condition. However, the implementation of condition-based PdM systems is challenging due to the large volume of data generated by the trucks, the inherent complexity of detecting failures through sensor data and the difficulties in finding cost-effective trade-offs in the solution's implementation. In this paper, we define and validate a condition-based PdM methodology built on the assumption that the wear-and-tear state of the monitored component can be represented as a monotonically non-decreasing time series. It involves selecting only the most recent observations from the time series and transforming them into a tabular format for classification using machine learning (ML) models designed for tabular data. Our results indicate that the proposed methodology reduces costs on the Scania Component X dataset compared to current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, while also simplifying the modeling process through AutoML.

</details>


### [170] [DynamicPTQ: Mitigating Activation Quantization Collapse via Residual-Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12487)
*Zimo Zhao,Maolin Wang,Bowen Yu,Bowen Liu,Xiao Han,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DynamicPTQ，一种动态后训练量化策略。研究发现大规模激活在残差流中呈现阶段式出现和消失的模式，导致静态变换平滑方法无法完全解决量化不稳定问题。DynamicPTQ通过识别对量化敏感的层，仅对这些层分配8位激活精度，其余保持4位，在LLaMA-2/3上提升了困惑度和零样本QA性能，吞吐量提升1.05-1.07倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练量化方法在将权重、激活和KV缓存都量化到4位时，面临大规模激活带来的量化误差放大问题。现有方法主要通过基于变换的平滑技术（如正交旋转和仿射缩放）来缓解，但忽略了残差流的跨层动态特性，导致静态变换无法完全解决动态量化不稳定性。

Method: 引入Jump Ratio和Historical Feature SNR两个指标来刻画残差流的跨层动态行为；提出DynamicPTQ策略，根据残差流动态识别量化敏感层，仅对这些敏感层分配8位激活精度，其余保持4位；该策略可直接集成到QuaRot、SpinQuant、FlatQuant等强基线中。

Result: 在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上，DynamicPTQ在W4A4KV4量化设置下持续提升了困惑度和零样本QA性能，同时实现了1.05到1.07倍的吞吐量提升，内存开销适中。

Conclusion: DynamicPTQ提供了一条实现稳健低比特LLM推理的实用路径，证明了考虑残差流动态特性对提升量化效果的关键作用。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for efficient large language model inference, but reliably quantizing activations remains challenging when weights, activations, and KV caches are all quantized to 4-bit precision. A key difficulty lies in massive activations, whose extreme values dominate the activation range and amplify quantization errors. State-of-the-art methods mainly mitigate massive activations through transformation-based smoothing, such as orthogonal rotations and affine scaling, but overlook the cross-layer dynamics of the residual stream. In this paper, we show that massive activations emerge and disappear in a phase-wise pattern across network depth, triggering large residual changes. These changes cause newly injected layer-wise updates to dominate the 4-bit quantization scale and weaken historical residual information. To characterize this behavior, we introduce Jump Ratio and Historical Feature SNR. This suggests that static transformation-based smoothing cannot fully resolve dynamic quantization instability caused by cross-layer residual changes. Based on this analysis, we propose DynamicPTQ, a Dynamic Post-Training Quantization policy for phase-aware mixed-precision activation quantization. DynamicPTQ identifies quantization-sensitive layers from residual-stream dynamics and assigns 8-bit activation precision only to these layers, while keeping weights, KV caches, and other activations in 4-bit precision. It can be directly integrated with strong PTQ baselines such as QuaRot, SpinQuant, and FlatQuant. Experiments on LLaMA-2 and LLaMA-3 show that DynamicPTQ consistently improves perplexity and zero-shot QA performance under W4A4KV4 quantization, while achieving 1.05 to 1.07 times throughput improvement with modest memory overhead. These results demonstrate a practical path toward robust low-bit LLM inference.

</details>


### [171] [A Stationary (and Therefore Compatible) Representation is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12488)
*Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Simone Ricci,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明d-Simplex固定分类器学习的平稳表示天然具有兼容性，并针对高阶依赖不足的问题，提出结合交叉熵与对比损失的凸组合方法，实现了模型更新时的无缝检索服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习兼容表示的目的是在模型更新时，特征表示可以互换使用。现有方法未能充分捕获模型更新间表示的高阶依赖关系。

Method: 使用d-Simplex固定分类器，通过交叉熵损失与对比损失的凸组合进行训练，该组合等价于在兼容性约束下的交叉熵学习。

Result: 实验证明该方法在模型顺序微调和替换场景下，无需重新处理图库图像即可实现不间断检索服务，性能达到最优。

Conclusion: 平稳表示可实现模型更新时的兼容性，所提方法有效捕获高阶依赖，在兼容性学习任务上取得最先进结果。

Abstract: Learning compatible representations aims to learn feature representations that can be used interchangeably over time whenever a model undergoes updates. In this paper, we demonstrate that stationary representations learned by d-Simplex fixed classifiers imply compatibility as in its formal definition. This result establishes a foundation for future works and can be directly exploited in practical learning scenarios. We address the challenge of learning compatibility using $d$-Simplex fixed classifiers when the model is sequentially fine-tuned. Learning according to a d-Simplex fixed classifier with the cross-entropy loss aligns feature distributions at the first-order statistics. Consequently, it may not fully capture higher-order dependencies in the representation between model updates. To address this issue, we demonstrate that training the model using a $d$-Simplex fixed classifier through a convex combination of the cross-entropy loss and a contrastive loss not only captures higher-order dependencies, but is also equivalent to learning with the cross-entropy under the compatibility constraints. We confirm our findings with extensive experiments also considering a new scenario where a pre-trained model is sequentially fine-tuned and occasionally replaced with an improved model. We show that stationary representations enable uninterrupted retrieval services (without reprocessing gallery images) while improving performance during model updates and replacements, achieving state-of-the-art. Code at https://github.com/miccunifi/iamcl2r.

</details>


### [172] [Robustness Verification of Recurrent Neural Networks with Abstraction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12490)
*Li-Jen Lin,Chih-Duo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RNN本地鲁棒性验证中因非线性松弛导致近似误差累积的问题，提出一种抽象-精化框架，通过对跨零预激活区间进行划分来消除主导松弛误差，并利用SHAP引导的时间步选择策略控制组合爆炸，实验证明在验证成功率和紧致性上优于纯抽象基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN的局部鲁棒性验证困难，因为非线性松弛引入的逼近误差会通过循环连接传播累积，导致线性界传播方法过于保守，尤其在预激活区间跨零时难以证明本应鲁棒的输入。

Method: 提出抽象-精化框架：1) 对跨零预激活区间进行划分，使ReLU精确化、tanh/sigmoid的线性包络更紧；2) 引入SHAP引导的时间步选择策略，按对验证目标的贡献度排序隐状态，仅精化最关键的时间步。

Result: 在CIFAR10和MNIST笔画基准上，验证成功率和鲁棒性边界紧致性均优于纯抽象基线方法，同时揭示了ReLU与tanh模型在运行时间上的明确权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的抽象-精化框架能有效缓解RNN验证中的近似误差累积问题，通过选择性精化策略在精度与计算开销之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Certified local robustness verification for recurrent neural networks (RNNs) is challenging because approximation errors introduced by nonlinear relaxations can propagate through recurrent connections and accumulate over time. As a result, scalable linear bound propagation methods often become overly conservative and fail to certify inputs that are in fact robust, especially when many pre-activation intervals cross zero. We propose an abstraction-refinement framework for RNN verification that partitions such intervals to remove the dominant relaxation error: on each refined branch, ReLU becomes exact, and smooth activations such as tanh and sigmoid admit substantially tighter linear envelopes. To control the combinatorial cost of splitting in long sequences, we introduce a SHAP-guided timestep selection strategy that ranks hidden states by their contribution to the verification objective and refines only the most critical timesteps in temporal order. Experiments on CIFAR10 and MNIST stroke benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in verification success and robustness-margin tightness over abstraction-only baselines, while exposing clear runtime trade-offs between ReLU and tanh models.

</details>


### [173] [Net-Ev$^2$: A Generative Simulator for Network Event Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12494)
*Guangyu Wang,Zhaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Net-Ev²，一个结合事件语义与网络拓扑结构的生成式模拟器，用于模拟扰动事件在网络中的传播演化，在四个大规模路网数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法同时建模事件的结构化属性与非结构化语义，也无法捕获网络拓扑结构来模拟事件演化过程。因此，作者提出一种能联合利用事件线索并保持网络拓扑结构的生成式模拟器。

Method: Net-Ev²包含两个阶段：结构引导的掩码预训练和拓扑感知的扩散过程。扩散去噪过程中采用类似U-Net的图下采样和上采样操作。推理时仅需自然语言事件输入即可生成模拟。

Result: 在四个大规模路网数据集上取得最优性能，并展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Net-Ev²能够有效模拟扰动事件在网络中的传播影响，填补了现有方法在事件语义与网络拓扑联合建模方面的空白。

Abstract: Reducing real-world trial and error has long been a central goal of decision making, and generative simulators advance this goal by modeling the evolution of future states. An even more challenging yet meaningful task is simulating how disturbance events (e.g., accidents) propagate their impacts across real-world networks. The existing approaches fall short of modeling both structured attributes and unstructured semantics of events, and capturing topological structures in simulating network event evolution. Therefore, we are motivated to propose Net-Ev$^2$ ($\underline{\textbf{Net}}$work $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$ent $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$olution), a novel generative simulator that jointly leverages event cues while preserving network topology in simulations. Specifically, the framework consists of two stages, namely structure-guided masked pre-training and topology-aware diffusion process, which is achieved by U-Net-like graph downsampling and upsampling during denoising. At inference time, Net-Ev$^2$ can generate simulations using natural-language event input only, with greater flexibility for practical usage. Furthermore, we introduce Net-Ev$^2$-6.5M, a multimodal benchmark of aligned event and network traffic data across four large-scale road networks, as well as a new topology-aware metric, namely JL-MMD, to evaluate topological fidelity in generated network dynamics. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization ability of Net-Ev$^2$. Code is made available at https://github.com/Guangyu4/Net-Ev-2.

</details>


### [174] [$μ$VLA: On Recurrent Memory for Partially Observable Manipulation in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12497)
*Egor Cherepanov,Nikita Kachaev,Daniil Zelezetsky,Aydar Bulatov,Artem Pshenitsyn,Yuri Kuratov,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制变量实验，在预训练VLA骨干网络中仅引入最小化的循环机制（可学习记忆token+截断BPTT），发现该简单方法在部分可观测任务中可将成功率从0.42提升至0.84，在完全可观测任务中不造成性能下降，厘清了“纯循环机制”的能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强型VLA模型同时引入了循环、检索、压缩、辅助目标、层次记忆或任务特定架构改造，导致循环本身对性能的贡献被其他组件混淆，无法判断纯循环机制的有效性。

Method: 在预训练VLA（OpenVLA-OFT）的Transformer中仅添加少量可学习记忆token（参数化为记忆宽度m、TBPTT长度K、更新规则），跨时间步携带并通过自注意力更新，端到端训练（截断BPTT），无辅助损失和架构改变，构成μVLA系列。

Result: MIKASA-Robo：训练集成功率0.42→0.84（最强设置），保留任务0.23 vs 无记忆基线0.07；LIBERO：最强变体平均成功率96.2%，完全可观测下无性能退化。

Conclusion: 校准了最小化骨干内循环的能力包络：识别出纯循环“足够用”和“需要额外记忆结构”的两种场景，为后续记忆增强VLA设计提供了基准参考。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions from the current observation, an assumption that fails under partial observability, where decisions depend on information no longer visible. Existing memory-augmented VLAs simultaneously introduce recurrence, retrieval, compression modules, auxiliary objectives, hierarchical memory, or task-specific architectural changes, so the contribution of recurrence itself remains entangled with surrounding machinery. We present a controlled isolation study of recurrence in a strong pretrained VLA backbone. Our formulation augments the transformer with a small set of learnable memory tokens carried across timesteps and updated through self-attention, trained end to end with truncated backpropagation through time, with no auxiliary losses and no architectural changes. We instantiate this as $μ$VLA, a family of OpenVLA-OFT variants parameterized by memory width m, TBPTT length K, and the memory update rule (cross-step gradients or a detached EMA), so that recurrence is the only varying factor. On MIKASA-Robo, $μ$VLA improves average success rate on five training tasks from 0.42 to 0.84 at the strongest setting and reaches 0.23 on held-out tasks with the same memory structure versus 0.07 for the memoryless baseline. On tasks requiring different memory structure, performance remains near baseline. On LIBERO, the strongest recurrent variant achieves 96.2% average success, indicating no regression under full observability. We interpret these results as a calibration of the capability envelope of minimal in-backbone recurrence, identifying the regime in which it is sufficient and the regime where additional memory structure is required. Demos and videos can be found in https://avanturist322.github.io/mu-vla/.

</details>


### [175] [Improving Crash Frequency Prediction from Simulated Traffic Conflicts Using Machine Learning Based Microsimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12500)
*Xian Liu,Carlo G. Prato,Gustav Markkula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于机器学习的驾驶行为模型在交通微观仿真中用于碰撞预测的效果。在利兹五个信号交叉口的实验中，ML模型生成的冲突经极值理论分析后能准确预测实际碰撞频率，而传统规则模型因缺乏校准效果不佳。直接使用ML模拟碰撞预测效果也差，说明ML模型能真实再现冲突但尚不能生成真实碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微观仿真多采用简化规则模型，虽能合理复现交通流但难以生成真实的冲突动力学，限制了碰撞预测精度。机器学习行为模型可直接从大规模轨迹数据中学习人类驾驶行为，有望提升仿真真实性和碰撞预测准确性，本研究旨在探究这一可能性。

Method: 在利兹五个真实信号交叉口，分别使用标准规则模型和先进ML模型进行交通微观仿真；利用二维碰撞时间指标识别仿真冲突；采用极值理论建模以预测碰撞频率；对比ML模型直接生成的模拟碰撞与真实碰撞数据。

Result: ML模型生成的冲突经极值理论分析后，碰撞预测与真实碰撞数据吻合；规则模型因缺乏位置特定校准无法产生有意义的预测；直接使用ML模拟碰撞预测真实碰撞效果较差。

Conclusion: ML行为模型在无需位置特定校准的情况下，通过冲突分析能有效提升碰撞预测准确性；当前ML模型能真实再现冲突但尚不能生成真实碰撞，为基于ML的微观仿真指明了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Traffic microsimulation combined with surrogate safety measures has increasingly been used as a proactive alternative to historical crash data for predicting crash frequency for current or planned road infrastructure designs. However, existing microsimulation-based safety studies have adopted simplified rule-based behaviour models, which reproduce traffic flow reasonably well but often fail to generate realistic conflict dynamics, limiting crash prediction accuracy. Recent advances in machine learning (ML)-based behaviour models offer a promising opportunity to potentially improve microsimulation realism and crash frequency predictions by learning human driving behaviour directly from large-scale trajectory datasets. To investigate this possibility, traffic microsimulation was conducted for five real-world signalised intersections in Leeds, UK, using both a standard rule-based model and a state-of-the-art ML model. Simulated vehicle trajectories were analysed using a two-dimensional Time-to-Collision metric to identify simulated conflicts, which were then modelled using Extreme Value Theory to predict crash frequency. Results show that conflicts from the ML model yielded crash predictions in line with the real-world crash data, whereas the rule-based model did not permit meaningful predictions, presumably due to a lack of model calibration to the specific simulated intersections. Directly using ML-generated simulated crashes to predict real-world crash frequency also yielded poor results, suggesting that while current ML models can realistically reproduce conflicts, they are not yet able to generate realistic crashes. Overall, the findings demonstrate that ML-based behaviour models are promising for improving crash prediction from simulated conflicts, without a need for location-specific model calibration, and suggest clear future directions for ML-based traffic microsimulation.

</details>


### [176] [Policy-driven Conformal Prediction for Trustworthy QoT Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12501)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Omran Ayoub,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Conformal QoT框架，结合统计保证的QoT估计与操作决策策略，在域偏移下实现可靠的光路可行性预测，将准确率从92%提升至99.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决域偏移下光路可行性预测可靠性不足的问题，现有方法准确率有限（92%），需要更鲁棒的QoT估计框架。

Method: 提出策略驱动的Conformal QoT框架，融合统计保证的QoT估计与操作决策策略。

Result: 在开放数据集上，准确率从92%提升至99.6%。

Conclusion: Conformal QoT框架能够有效提升域偏移下光路可行性预测的可靠性，显著提高预测准确率。

Abstract: We propose Conformal QoT, a policy-driven framework that combines statistically guaranteed QoT estimation with operational decision policies, enabling reliable lightpath-feasibility predictions under domain shift and improving accuracy from 92\% to 99.6\% on open datasets.

</details>


### [177] [Dolph2Vec: Self-Supervised Representations of Dolphin Vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12503)
*Chiara Semenzin,Faadil Mustun,Roberto Dessi,Pierre Orhan,Alexis Emanuelli,Yair Lakretz,Gonzalo de Polavieja,German Sumbre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dolph2Vec，首个针对海豚声音的大规模自监督学习模型，在五年纵向数据集上训练，显著优于通用基线模型，并能捕捉可解释的声学单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物声学自监督模型追求跨物种泛化，但缺乏对特定物种个体通信系统精细结构的建模能力。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 收集了五年以上的海豚纵向录音数据，基于Wav2Vec2.0架构，训练了物种特异性自监督模型Dolph2Vec。

Result: 在签名哨声分类和哨声检测两项任务上显著优于通用基线，学到的嵌入和码本结构能解释海豚哨声类别及亚结构。

Conclusion: 自监督学习可以作为探索动物通信研究假设的科学工具，既能作为模型也能作为分析方法。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has opened new opportunities in bioacoustics by enabling scalable modeling of animal vocalizations without the need for expensive manual annotation. However, current SSL models in this domain prioritize broad generalization across species and are not optimized for uncovering the fine-grained structure of individual communication systems. In this work, we collect and release a novel dataset of over five years of longitudinal recordings, from five known dolphins in a semi-naturalistic marine environment, an unprecedented resource for studying dolphin communication. We adapt the Wav2Vec2.0 Baevski et al. (2020) architecture to this domain and introduce Dolph2Vec, the first large-scale, species-specific SSL model trained exclusively on this data. We benchmark our model on two biologically relevant tasks: signature whistle classification and whistle detection. Dolph2Vec significantly outperforms general-purpose baselines in both tasks. Beyond performance, we show that learned embeddings and codebook structure capture interpretable acoustic units aligned with dolphin whistle categories and possibly sub-whistle structure, enabling fine-grained analysis of communication patterns. Our findings demonstrate how SSL can serve as both a model and a scientific tool to explore hypotheses in animal communication research.

</details>


### [178] [Boosting Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12505)
*Pengwei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DPOP方法，在DPO基础上增加对参考模型贪心响应的门控惩罚，显著提升偏好优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO等离线偏好优化方法仅使用数据集中已有的chosen和rejected响应，忽略了参考模型自身生成响应的有用信号。

Method: 在DPO损失基础上增加一个门控惩罚项，仅在当前策略对偏好响应（chosen）的似然仍低于非偏好响应（rejected）时激活，惩罚参考模型的贪心生成响应。

Result: 在AlpacaEval 2.0上，DPOP在Llama-3-8b-it和Gemma-2-9b-it上分别比DPO、SimPO、AlphaDPO等基线提升了5.3%和4.4%的长度控制胜率。

Conclusion: DPOP通过利用参考模型响应信号有效改进了偏好优化，且SimNPO风格的长度归一化惩罚优于NPO和token级unlikelihood。

Abstract: Offline preference optimization has become a practical substitute for reinforcement learning from human feedback, but pairwise objectives such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and its variants use only the chosen and rejected responses stored in a static dataset. This leaves a useful signal unused: the response that the reference model itself would generate for the same prompt. We propose Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization (DPOP), a simple extension of DPO that augments the base preference loss with a gated penalty on reference-greedy responses. DPOP activates this penalty only when the current policy still assigns a lower likelihood to the preferred response than to the rejected response. On AlpacaEval 2.0, DPOP improves length-controlled win rate over DPO, SimPO, and AlphaDPO on both Llama-3-8b-it and Gemma-2-9b-it, achieving relative gains of 5.3\% and 4.4\% over baselines on the two models, respectively. Ablations further show that a SimNPO-style length-normalized penalty is stronger than NPO and token-level unlikelihood in this setting.

</details>


### [179] [Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation: Post-Training Without Rubric Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12507)
*MohammadHossein Rezaei,Anas Mahmoud,Zihao Wang,Utkarsh Tyagi,Advait Gosai,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Aakash Sabharwal,Bing Liu,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RGSD是一种无验证器的训练方法，通过将基于规则的教师蒸馏到学生模型中，用密集的逐token学习信号替代稀疏的轨迹级奖励，在医疗和科学领域实现了与基于评判器的GRPO相当的规则满意度，同时显著降低了训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于规则的训练方法依赖LLM验证器打分，存在训练开销大、易受验证器偏差影响、反馈信号稀疏等问题，亟需一种无验证器的高效替代方案。

Method: 提出Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation (RGSD)，以基策略模型在规则条件化下作为教师，蒸馏知识给无条件化的学生模型，实现逐token的密集学习信号，完全移除LLM评判器。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B)和Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B)模型上，RGSD达到与基于评判器的GRPO相当的规则满意度，每个提示仅需一次on-policy rollout且无需训练时验证器调用。

Conclusion: RGSD可作为验证器成本或可靠性成为瓶颈时的互补性无验证器替代方案；原始规则比自生成参考响应提供更强的教师增强信号，但更强的GRPO评判器在某些设置下仍优于RGSD。

Abstract: Rubrics have emerged as an alternative to RLVR in open-ended domains where a single ground-truth final answer is not available. Existing rubric-based training methods rely on an LLM verifier that scores each rollout against rubrics. This introduces substantial training-time overhead, exposes optimization to verifier-specific biases, and reduces rubric feedback to a sparse end-of-trajectory signal. We propose Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation (RGSD), a verifier-free training method in which the base policy, conditioned on the rubric, serves as the teacher for the unconditioned student. RGSD distills the rubric-conditioned teacher distribution into the student token-by-token, replacing sparse trajectory-level rewards with dense per-token learning signals and removing the LLM judge from the training loop entirely. Across Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B) and Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B) models on medical and science domains, RGSD achieves rubric satisfaction comparable to judge-based GRPO while using one on-policy rollout per prompt and no training-time verifier calls. Ablations show that raw rubrics provide a stronger teacher enrichment signal than self-generated reference responses, while a stronger GRPO judge can outperform RGSD in some settings, positioning RGSD as a complementary verifier-free alternative when verifier cost or reliability is the bottleneck.

</details>
